# Graph Report - local-llm-server  (2026-09-25)

## Corpus Check
- 1590 files · ~2,304,667 words
- Verdict: corpus is large enough that graph structure adds value.
- Unclassified: 55 file(s) not represented in the graph (top: (none) 17, .bat 5, .css 5)

## Summary
- 33747 nodes · 68637 edges · 1362 communities (1183 shown, 179 thin omitted)
- Extraction: 90% EXTRACTED · 10% INFERRED · 0% AMBIGUOUS · INFERRED: 6708 edges (avg confidence: 0.93)
- Token cost: 0 input · 0 output

## Graph Freshness
- Built from commit: `50ed49ef`
- Run `git rev-parse HEAD` and compare to check if the graph is stale.
- Run `graphify update .` after code changes (no API cost).
